# 02 — Requirement simplification matrix

> Generated by `tools/reqaudit/assemble_simplification.py` from `data/`. Do not edit by hand.

This is the complete matrix of dispositions, with aggregate estimates of avoidable human work, customer interactions and configuration.

**How to read the aggregates.**
- They are each pass's [A] estimates for a 10,000-account sponsor.
- They are not additive across slices without double-counting risk, because some work appears in more than one slice. The adversarial review discusses this in [22](22-ADVERSARIAL-REVIEW.md).
- The grants slice's literal reading includes manual 180-day revalidation and per-grant review that existing refinements already remove, which is why its share is so large.

**Cross-layer duplication pattern.** The same obligation typically appears 3-5 times: baseline (ADM/UNB-PART/UNB-SUCC) -> landscape wave (UNB-LS/UNB-WF/UNB-GAP) -> REQ-X overlay -> reconciliation record (UNB-RCN) or refinement (RR) -> discovery record (UNB-XIN). Examples: succession transition (ADM-022, UNB-WF-013, UNB-GAP-015, REQ-X-068, UNB-SUCC-008, UNB-RCN-ENT-004 -> RR-UNB-SUCC-008-r1); reconsideration (UNB-RCN-ENT-016, UNB-RCN-WF-018 -> UNB-XIN-CLM-001, with conflicting parameters, SPF-ENT-002); dormancy (ADM-024, UNB-WF-015, UNB-LS-015, UNB-RCN-ENT-005 -> RR-UNB-WF-015-r1).

**Contradictions inside clusters:** TVR-CON-003 vs RR-REQ-X-009-r1 (donor receivable, SPF-ENT-003); UNB-RCN-WF-018 vs UNB-XIN-CLM-001 (window/outcomes, SPF-ENT-002); RR-REQ-X-048-r1 superseded by r2 but both listed (XIC-SVC-001 resolved it).
